{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"cottons","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A fibrous substance:","A soft, fibrous, usually white substance consisting of fine hairs, especially the substance around the seeds of a plant of genus Gossypium."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A fibrous substance:","Any similar soft, fibrous, white substance of fine hairs, of any origin."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A plant source:","A plant of genus Gossypium, used as a source of such fiber."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A plant source:","Any plant that encases its seed in a thin fiber that is harvested and used as a fabric or cloth."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A manufactured product of such a substance:","Textiles made from the fiber harvested from a cotton plant, especially Gossypium."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"],"topics":["business","manufacturing","textiles"]},{"glosses":["A manufactured product of such a substance:","An item of clothing made from such textiles."],"tags":["countable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A manufactured product of such a substance:","A small piece of cotton used as a filter when drawing up a drug into a syringe."],"qualifier":"recreational drugs","tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A manufactured product of such a substance:","Thread of such fiber."],"tags":["uncountable","usually"]},{"glosses":["A manufactured product of such a substance:","A wick of a candle."],"tags":["archaic","uncountable","usually"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈkɒt.n̩/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkʰɒt.n̩]","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˈkɑt.n̩/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"[ˈkɑ.ʔn̩]","tags":["General-American"]}],"upstream_line":35849,"upstream_offset":249640426,"upstream_record_sha256":"34bfd569d50e798b0cd4fc5c340622a1452b8add2d83fbb64029d39d50e5d548","word":"cotton"}

{"forms":[{"form":"knees","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"kneen","tags":["dialectal","obsolete","plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"Penny was wearing a miniskirt, so she skinned her exposed knees when she fell.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["In humans, the joint or the region of the joint in the middle part of the leg between the thigh and the shank."],"topics":["anatomy","medicine","sciences"]},{"glosses":["In the horse and allied animals, the carpal joint, corresponding to the wrist in humans."]},{"glosses":["In birds:","The tarsal joint, uniting the tarsometatarsus and the tibiotarsus (homologous to the human ankle)."],"qualifier":"non-technical"},{"glosses":["In birds:","The joint uniting the tibiotarsus and the femur, typically hidden beneath plumage."]},{"glosses":["The part of a garment that covers the knee."]},{"glosses":["A piece of timber or metal formed with an angle somewhat in the shape of the human knee when bent."],"topics":["business","manufacturing","shipbuilding"]},{"glosses":["An act of kneeling, especially to show respect or courtesy."],"tags":["archaic"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the knee of a graph","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any knee-shaped item or sharp angle in a line; an inflection point."]},{"glosses":["A blow made with the knee; a kneeing."]},{"glosses":["The presence of a parent etc., where a young child acquires early knowledge."],"tags":["figuratively"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"nē","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/niː/","tags":["UK"]},{"ipa":"/ni/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/kniː/","tags":["Early","Modern"]}],"upstream_line":24866,"upstream_offset":170922056,"upstream_record_sha256":"68c4702ef886474235397290822c0ac2a701d5761543f9c6d39bf340a8fbe44f","word":"knee"}
